     1→class DashboardController < ApplicationController
     2→  def index
     3→    @sandboxes = policy_scope(Sandbox).includes(:user, :routes).order(:name)
     4→  end
     5→
     6→  def card
     7→    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).includes(:user, :routes).find(params[:id])
     8→    authorize sandbox
     9→    render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(dom_id(sandbox), partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: sandbox })
    10→  end
    11→
    12→  def stats
    13→    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])
    14→    authorize sandbox, :stats?
    15→
    16→    if sandbox.status == "running" && sandbox.container_id.present?
    17→      container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
    18→      raw = container.stats(stream: false)
    19→
    20→      # Handle case where container is shutting down and returns nil/incomplete stats
    21→      if raw.present?
    22→        networks = raw["networks"] || {}
    23→        net_rx = networks.values.sum { |n| n["rx_bytes"] || 0 }
    24→        net_tx = networks.values.sum { |n| n["tx_bytes"] || 0 }
    25→
    26→        blkio = raw.dig("blkio_stats", "io_service_bytes_recursive") || []
    27→        disk_read = blkio.select { |e| e["op"]&.downcase == "read" }.sum { |e| e["value"] || 0 }
    28→        disk_write = blkio.select { |e| e["op"]&.downcase == "write" }.sum { |e| e["value"] || 0 }
    29→
    30→        @stats = {
    31→          cpu_percent: calculate_cpu_percent(raw),
    32→          memory_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "usage") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
    33→          memory_limit_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "limit") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
    34→          net_rx: net_rx,
    35→          net_tx: net_tx,
    36→          disk_read: disk_read,
    37→          disk_write: disk_write,
    38→          pids: raw.dig("pids_stats", "current") || 0
    39→        }
    40→      end
    41→    end
    42→
    43→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: @stats, sandbox: sandbox }
    44→  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound
    45→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: nil, sandbox: nil }
    46→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
    47→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: nil, sandbox: sandbox }
    48→  end
    49→
    50→  private
    51→
    52→  def calculate_cpu_percent(stats)
    53→    cpu_delta = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "cpu_usage", "total_usage").to_f -
    54→                stats.dig("precpu_stats", "cpu_usage", "total_usage").to_f
    55→    system_delta = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "system_cpu_usage").to_f -
    56→                   stats.dig("precpu_stats", "system_cpu_usage").to_f
    57→    num_cpus = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "online_cpus") || 1
    58→
    59→    return 0.0 if system_delta.zero?
    60→    ((cpu_delta / system_delta) * num_cpus * 100.0).round(1)
    61→  end
    62→end
    63→

> AGENT

`dom_id` is a view helper — it's not available in controllers. The fix is to use `ActionView::RecordIdentifier.dom_id(sandbox)` or just call `helpers.dom_id(sandbox)`.

> AGENT

The issue was that `dom_id` is a view helper, not available directly in controllers. `helpers.dom_id(sandbox)` proxies through to the view helper module. This will correctly generate the DOM ID for the Turbo Stream replace target.

     1→class VncManager
     2→  DATA_DIR = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR", "/data")
     3→  NOVNC_IMAGE = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_NOVNC_IMAGE", "gotget/novnc:latest")
     4→  NETWORK_NAME = "sandcastle-web"
     5→  DYNAMIC_DIR = File.join(DATA_DIR, "traefik", "dynamic")
     6→
     7→  class Error < StandardError; end
     8→
     9→  # Opens a web-based VNC session for the given sandbox.
    10→  # Returns the URL path to the noVNC session.
    11→  def open(sandbox:)
    12→    raise Error, "Sandbox is not running" unless sandbox.status == "running"
    13→    raise Error, "Sandbox has no container" if sandbox.container_id.blank?
    14→
    15→    user = sandbox.user
    16→    container_name = vnc_container_name(sandbox)
    17→
    18→    # Idempotent: if noVNC container already running, return URL
    19→    if container_running?(container_name)
    20→      return vnc_url(sandbox)
    21→    end
    22→
    23→    pull_image
    24→    ensure_network
    25→    connect_sandbox_to_network(sandbox)
    26→
    27→    # Write Traefik config early so it has time to detect the new route
    28→    write_traefik_config(sandbox)
    29→
    30→    create_novnc_container(sandbox: sandbox, user: user)
    31→
    32→    # Give Traefik time to detect the new route configuration
    33→    sleep 2
    34→
    35→    vnc_url(sandbox)
    36→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    37→    raise Error, "Failed to open browser: #{e.message}"
    38→  rescue SystemCallError => e
    39→    raise Error, "Failed to open browser: #{e.message}"
    40→  end
    41→
    42→  # Closes the web VNC session for the given sandbox.
    43→  def close(sandbox:)
    44→    container_name = vnc_container_name(sandbox)
    45→
    46→    # Stop and remove noVNC container
    47→    begin
    48→      container = Docker::Container.get(container_name)
    49→      container.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
    50→      container.delete(force: true)
    51→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
    52→      # Already gone
    53→    end
    54→
    55→    # Delete Traefik config
    56→    delete_traefik_config(sandbox)
    57→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    58→    Rails.logger.error("VncManager: close failed for #{sandbox.full_name}: #{e.message}")
    59→  end
    60→
    61→  # Returns true if the noVNC container is running AND its HTTP server is ready.
    62→  # The container_running? check alone is insufficient — the websockify process
    63→  # may not have bound to port 6080 yet when Docker reports the container as running.
    64→  def active?(sandbox:)
    65→    container_name = vnc_container_name(sandbox)
    66→    return false unless container_running?(container_name)
    67→
    68→    TCPSocket.new(container_name, 6080).close
    69→    true
    70→  rescue Errno::ECONNREFUSED, Errno::ETIMEDOUT, SocketError
    71→    false
    72→  end
    73→
    74→  # Removes orphaned noVNC containers whose sandbox no longer exists or is not running.
    75→  def cleanup_orphaned
    76→    Docker::Container.all(all: true).each do |container|
    77→      name = container.info.dig("Names")&.first&.delete_prefix("/")
    78→      next unless name&.start_with?("sc-vnc-")
    79→
    80→      labels = container.info["Labels"] || {}
    81→      sandbox_id = labels["sandcastle.sandbox_id"]&.to_i
    82→
    83→      sandbox = sandbox_id ? Sandbox.find_by(id: sandbox_id) : nil
    84→      should_remove = sandbox.nil? || sandbox.status != "running"
    85→
    86→      if should_remove
    87→        container.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
    88→        container.delete(force: true)
    89→        Rails.logger.info("VncManager: removed orphaned noVNC container #{name}")
    90→
    91→        # Clean up Traefik config if we have a sandbox_id
    92→        if sandbox_id
    93→          config_path = File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "vnc-#{sandbox_id}.yml")
    94→          File.delete(config_path) if File.exist?(config_path)
    95→        end
    96→      end
    97→    end
    98→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    99→    Rails.logger.error("VncManager: orphan cleanup failed: #{e.message}")
   100→  end
   101→
   102→  private
   103→
   104→  def vnc_container_name(sandbox)
   105→    "sc-vnc-#{sandbox.full_name}"
   106→  end
   107→
   108→  def vnc_url(sandbox)
   109→    id = sandbox.id
   110→    # Strip prefix is /vnc/{id}, so all requests under /vnc/{id}/* are forwarded
   111→    # to the noVNC container with the prefix removed. This keeps noVNC's relative
   112→    # asset paths (package.json, core/*.js) resolving correctly within the prefix.
   113→    # The ?path= tells noVNC where to connect the WebSocket; Traefik strips
   114→    # /vnc/{id} leaving /websockify which websockify handles.
   115→    "/vnc/#{id}/vnc.html?path=vnc/#{id}/websockify"
   116→  end
   117→
   118→  def container_running?(name)
   119→    container = Docker::Container.get(name)
   120→    container.json.dig("State", "Running") == true
   121→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   122→    false
   123→  end
   124→
   125→  def pull_image
   126→    Docker::Image.get(NOVNC_IMAGE)
   127→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   128→    Docker::Image.create("fromImage" => NOVNC_IMAGE)
   129→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
   130→    raise Error, "Failed to pull #{NOVNC_IMAGE} — check network connectivity"
   131→  end
   132→
   133→  def ensure_network
   134→    Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   135→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   136→    Docker::Network.create(NETWORK_NAME, "Driver" => "bridge")
   137→  end
   138→
   139→  def connect_sandbox_to_network(sandbox)
   140→    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?
   141→
   142→    network = Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   143→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   144→
   145→    networks = container.json.dig("NetworkSettings", "Networks") || {}
   146→    return if networks.key?(NETWORK_NAME)
   147→
   148→    network.connect(sandbox.container_id)
   149→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   150→    # Container no longer exists - sync job will fix the DB state
   151→    raise Error, "Sandbox container not found. Please refresh and try again."
   152→  end
   153→
   154→  def create_novnc_container(sandbox:, user:)
   155→    container_name = vnc_container_name(sandbox)
   156→
   157→    # Remove any existing container with this name
   158→    begin
   159→      old = Docker::Container.get(container_name)
   160→      old.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
   161→      old.delete(force: true)
   162→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   163→      # No existing container
   164→    end
   165→
   166→    host_config = {
   167→      "NetworkMode" => NETWORK_NAME,
   168→      "RestartPolicy" => { "Name" => "no" },
   169→      "Memory" => 128 * 1024 * 1024, # 128MB — noVNC static files + websockify proxy
   170→      "NanoCpus" => 250_000_000 # 0.25 CPU
   171→    }
   172→
   173→    # Run the noVNC image using its built-in launch.sh, which starts both the
   174→    # mini-webserver (noVNC HTML/JS) and websockify (WS→RFB proxy) on port 6080.
   175→    # Traefik routes everything under /vnc/{id} to this container; after
   176→    # stripPrefix the websockify path becomes /websockify as noVNC expects.
   177→    container = Docker::Container.create(
   178→      "name" => container_name,
   179→      "Image" => NOVNC_IMAGE,
   180→      "Cmd" => [ "--vnc", "#{sandbox.full_name}:5900", "--listen", "6080" ],
   181→      "HostConfig" => host_config,
   182→      "Labels" => {
   183→        "sandcastle.sandbox_id" => sandbox.id.to_s,
   184→        "sandcastle.role" => "novnc"
   185→      }
   186→    )
   187→
   188→    container.start
   189→    container
   190→  end
   191→
   192→  def write_traefik_config(sandbox)
   193→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(DYNAMIC_DIR)
   194→
   195→    host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")
   196→    id = sandbox.id
   197→    container_name = vnc_container_name(sandbox)
   198→    sandbox_name   = sandbox.full_name
   199→
   200→    base_rule = if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   201→      "HostRegexp(`.+`) && PathPrefix(`/vnc/#{id}`)"
   202→    else
   203→      "Host(`#{host}`) && PathPrefix(`/vnc/#{id}`)"
   204→    end
   205→
   206→    # Single router: all traffic under /vnc/{id} goes to the noVNC sidecar.
   207→    # The noVNC container (gotget/novnc launch.sh) serves static HTML/JS on /
   208→    # and proxies WebSocket VNC frames via websockify on /websockify, both on
   209→    # port 6080. stripPrefix removes /vnc/{id} before forwarding, so noVNC's
   210→    # relative asset paths (package.json, core/*.js, /websockify) resolve correctly.
   211→    config = {
   212→      "http" => {
   213→        "routers" => {
   214→          "vnc-#{id}" => {
   215→            "rule" => base_rule,
   216→            "service" => "vnc-#{id}",
   217→            "entryPoints" => [ "websecure" ],
   218→            "tls" => tls_config,
   219→            "middlewares" => [ "vnc-auth-#{id}", "vnc-stripprefix-#{id}" ],
   220→            "priority" => 100
   221→          }
   222→        },
   223→        "middlewares" => {
   224→          "vnc-auth-#{id}" => {
   225→            "forwardAuth" => {
   226→              "address" => "http://sandcastle-web:80/vnc/auth",
   227→              "trustForwardHeader" => true
   228→            }
   229→          },
   230→          "vnc-stripprefix-#{id}" => {
   231→            "stripPrefix" => {
   232→              "prefixes" => [ "/vnc/#{id}" ]
   233→            }
   234→          }
   235→        },
   236→        "services" => {
   237→          "vnc-#{id}" => {
   238→            "loadBalancer" => {
   239→              "servers" => [ { "url" => "http://#{container_name}:6080" } ]
   240→            }
   241→          }
   242→        }
   243→      }
   244→    }
   245→
   246→    File.write(File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "vnc-#{id}.yml"), config.to_yaml)
   247→  end
   248→
   249→  def tls_config
   250→    if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   251→      {}
   252→    else
   253→      { "certResolver" => "letsencrypt" }
   254→    end
   255→  end
   256→
   257→  def delete_traefik_config(sandbox)
   258→    path = File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "vnc-#{sandbox.id}.yml")
   259→    File.delete(path) if File.exist?(path)
   260→  end
   261→end
   262→

     1→class VncController < ApplicationController
     2→  allow_unauthenticated_access only: :auth
     3→  skip_before_action :require_password_change, only: :auth
     4→  skip_forgery_protection only: :open
     5→
     6→  def open
     7→    sandbox = find_sandbox
     8→
     9→    VncManager.new.open(sandbox: sandbox)
    10→    redirect_to vnc_wait_sandbox_path(sandbox), status: :see_other
    11→  rescue VncManager::Error => e
    12→    redirect_to root_path, alert: e.message
    13→  end
    14→
    15→  def wait
    16→    @sandbox = find_sandbox
    17→    id = @sandbox.id
    18→    @vnc_url = vnc_redirect_url("/vnc/#{id}/vnc.html?path=vnc/#{id}/websockify")
    19→  end
    20→
    21→  def status
    22→    sandbox = find_sandbox
    23→    ready = VncManager.new.active?(sandbox: sandbox)
    24→    render json: { status: ready ? "ready" : "waiting" }
    25→  end
    26→
    27→  def close
    28→    sandbox = find_sandbox
    29→
    30→    VncManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
    31→    redirect_to root_path, notice: "Browser session closed"
    32→  rescue VncManager::Error => e
    33→    redirect_to root_path, alert: e.message
    34→  end
    35→
    36→  private
    37→
    38→  def find_sandbox
    39→    if Current.user.admin?
    40→      Sandbox.active.find(params[:id])
    41→    else
    42→      Current.user.sandboxes.active.find(params[:id])
    43→    end
    44→  end
    45→
    46→  # Build the full VNC URL. In production, Traefik is the entry point
    47→  # so a relative path works. In local dev (selfsigned TLS), Rails may be
    48→  # accessed directly on a different port, so we need an absolute URL.
    49→  def vnc_redirect_url(path)
    50→    base = ENV["SANDCASTLE_VNC_URL"] || ENV["SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL"]
    51→    base ? "#{base}#{path}" : path
    52→  end
    53→
    54→  public
    55→
    56→  # Called by Traefik forwardAuth. Returns 200 to allow, or redirects to
    57→  # login (which Traefik passes through to the browser).
    58→  def auth
    59→    forwarded_uri = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Uri"] || ""
    60→    match = forwarded_uri.match(%r{/vnc/(\d+)})
    61→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless match
    62→
    63→    session_record = find_session_by_cookie
    64→    unless session_record
    65→      # Build the original VNC URL from Traefik's forwarded headers
    66→      # so the user returns here after logging in.
    67→      proto = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Proto"] || "https"
    68→      host  = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Host"] || request.host_with_port
    69→      original_url = "#{proto}://#{host}#{forwarded_uri}"
    70→      session[:return_to_after_authenticating] = original_url
    71→      redirect_to new_session_url, allow_other_host: true
    72→      return
    73→    end
    74→
    75→    user = session_record.user
    76→    sandbox = Sandbox.active.find_by(id: match[1].to_i)
    77→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless sandbox && (sandbox.user_id == user.id || user.admin?)
    78→
    79→    head :ok
    80→  end
    81→end
    82→

    20→        </svg>

    34→    var vncUrl = "<%= j @vnc_url %>";
    35→    var elapsed = 0;
    36→    var timeout = 30;
    37→
    38→    var poll = setInterval(function() {
    39→      elapsed++;
    40→
    41→      if (elapsed > timeout) {
    42→        clearInterval(poll);
    43→        document.getElementById("connecting").classList.add("hidden");
    44→        document.getElementById("timeout-error").classList.remove("hidden");
    45→        return;
    46→      }
    47→
    48→      fetch(statusUrl, { headers: { "Accept": "application/json" } })
    49→        .then(function(r) { return r.json(); })
    50→        .then(function(data) {
    51→          if (data.status === "ready") {
    52→            clearInterval(poll);
    53→            window.location.href = vncUrl;
    54→          }
    55→        })
    56→        .catch(function() {});
    57→    }, 1000);
    58→  })();
    59→</script>

     1→# LOCAL.md
     2→
     3→Local development environment guide for Sandcastle. Use this file when debugging locally with Docker Compose.
     4→
     5→## Overview
     6→
     7→Sandcastle has two local deployment modes:
     8→
     9→1. **`mise run deploy:local`** — Production-like environment (builds production image, uses `docker-compose.local.yml`)
    10→2. **`mise run deploy:dev`** — Development environment with live reload (mounts source code, uses `docker-compose.dev.yml`)
    11→
    12→Both modes run the full stack (Traefik, PostgreSQL, Rails web, Solid Queue worker) in Docker containers.
    13→
    14→## Prerequisites
    15→
    16→- Docker with Sysbox runtime installed
    17→- `mise` installed (`brew install mise`)
    18→- `sandcastle-web` network created: `docker network create sandcastle-web`
    19→- Self-signed certificates (auto-generated on first run or created manually)
    20→
    21→## Quick Start
    22→
    23→### Production-like Local Environment
    24→
    25→```bash
    26→# Start (builds image and runs)
    27→mise run deploy:local
    28→
    29→# Access at:
    30→#   - https://sandcastle.local:8443 (via Traefik, recommended)
    31→#   - http://sandcastle.local:8080 (via Traefik)
    32→
    33→# Stop
    34→mise run deploy:local:down
    35→
    36→# Reset (remove all volumes and containers, rebuild)
    37→mise run deploy:local:reset
    38→
    39→# View logs
    40→mise run deploy:local:logs
    41→```
    42→
    43→### Development Environment (Live Reload)
    44→
    45→```bash
    46→# Start (mounts source code)
    47→mise run deploy:dev
    48→
    49→# Access at:
    50→#   - https://localhost:8443
    51→#   - http://localhost:8080
    52→
    53→# Stop
    54→mise run deploy:dev:down
    55→
    56→# Reset
    57→mise run deploy:dev:reset
    58→```
    59→
    60→## Commands
    61→
    62→### Starting the Environment
    63→
    64→```bash
    65→# Production-like (full build)
    66→mise run deploy:local
    67→
    68→# Development (live reload)
    69→mise run deploy:dev
    70→```
    71→
    72→### Stopping
    73→
    74→```bash
    75→# Production-like
    76→mise run deploy:local:down
    77→
    78→# Development
    79→mise run deploy:dev:down
    80→
    81→# Alternative: stop all containers manually
    82→docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml down
    83→docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml down
    84→```
    85→
    86→### Resetting (Clean Slate)
    87→
    88→The reset commands remove WeTTY containers, Tailscale sidecars, all volumes, and orphaned containers:
    89→
    90→```bash
    91→# Production-like
    92→mise run deploy:local:reset
    93→
    94→# Development
    95→mise run deploy:dev:reset
    96→```
    97→
    98→This is useful when:
    99→- Database is in a bad state
   100→- Volumes are corrupted
   101→- You want a fresh start
   102→
   103→**Warning:** This deletes all data including database, user sandboxes, and Traefik config.
   104→
   105→### Viewing Logs
   106→
   107→```bash
   108→# Tail all logs (production-like)
   109→mise run deploy:local:logs
   110→
   111→# Tail all logs (development)
   112→docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml logs -f
   113→
   114→# Specific service
   115→docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml logs -f web
   116→docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml logs -f worker
   117→
   118→# Last 100 lines
   119→docker logs sandcastle-web --tail 100
   120→
   121→# Follow logs for a specific container
   122→docker logs -f sandcastle-web
   123→docker logs -f sandcastle-worker
   124→```
   125→
   126→## Inspecting the Environment
   127→
   128→### Container Status
   129→
   130→```bash
   131→# List all Sandcastle containers
   132→docker ps -a | grep sandcastle
   133→
   134→# List all containers (including sandboxes, WeTTY, Tailscale)
   135→docker ps -a
   136→
   137→# Filter by name pattern
   138→docker ps -a --filter "name=sc-wetty-"
   139→docker ps -a --filter "name=sc-ts-"
   140→```
   141→
   142→### Database Access
   143→
   144→```bash
   145→# Enter PostgreSQL container
   146→docker exec -it sandcastle_postgres_1 psql -U sandcastle -d sandcastle_production
   147→
   148→# Or for dev environment
   149→docker exec -it sandcastle_postgres_1 psql -U sandcastle -d sandcastle_development
   150→
   151→# Run a query
   152→docker exec -it sandcastle_postgres_1 psql -U sandcastle -d sandcastle_production -c "SELECT * FROM users;"
   153→
   154→# Check database size
   155→docker exec -it sandcastle_postgres_1 psql -U sandcastle -d sandcastle_production -c "\l+"
   156→```
   157→
   158→### Rails Console
   159→
   160→```bash
   161→# Production-like environment
   162→docker exec -it sandcastle-web ./bin/rails console
   163→
   164→# Development environment (same command)
   165→docker exec -it sandcastle-web ./bin/rails console
   166→```
   167→
   168→### Inspect Volumes
   169→
   170→```bash
   171→# List volumes
   172→docker volume ls | grep sandcastle
   173→
   174→# Inspect a volume
   175→docker volume inspect sandcastle_sandcastle-data
   176→docker volume inspect sandcastle_pgdata
   177→
   178→# Browse volume contents (via temporary container)
   179→docker run --rm -v sandcastle_sandcastle-data:/data -it busybox sh
   180→# Inside container: ls -la /data
   181→```
   182→
   183→### Network Inspection
   184→
   185→```bash
   186→# List networks
   187→docker network ls | grep sandcastle
   188→
   189→# Inspect the bridge network
   190→docker network inspect sandcastle-web
   191→
   192→# Check what containers are on the network
   193→docker network inspect sandcastle-web -f '{{range .Containers}}{{.Name}} {{end}}'
   194→```
   195→
   196→### Traefik Dashboard
   197→
   198→Traefik doesn't expose the dashboard by default. To view routing config:
   199→
   200→```bash
   201→# Inspect Traefik dynamic config
   202→docker exec -it sandcastle_traefik_1 cat /data/dynamic/rails.yml
   203→
   204→# View all dynamic configs
   205→docker exec -it sandcastle_traefik_1 ls -la /data/dynamic/
   206→```
   207→
   208→### Docker Socket Access
   209→
   210→The web and worker containers need Docker socket access to manage sandbox containers:
   211→
   212→```bash
   213→# Check if socket is mounted
   214→docker inspect sandcastle-web | grep -A 5 Mounts | grep docker.sock
   215→
   216→# Test Docker access from inside the container
   217→docker exec sandcastle-web docker ps
   218→```
   219→
   220→## Environment Variables
   221→
   222→### Production-like (`docker-compose.local.yml`)
   223→
   224→| Variable | Default | Description |
   225→|----------|---------|-------------|
   226→| `BUILD_VERSION` | `dev` | Version tag (auto-set from git) |
   227→| `BUILD_GIT_SHA` | `local` | Git commit SHA (auto-set) |
   228→| `BUILD_GIT_DIRTY` | `false` | Whether working tree is dirty |
   229→| `BUILD_DATE` | current | Build timestamp |
   230→| `SECRET_KEY_BASE` | `deadbeef` | Rails secret (change for production) |
   231→| `DB_PASSWORD` | `sandcastle` | PostgreSQL password |
   232→| `DOCKER_GID` | `0` | Docker socket group ID |
   233→
   234→### Development (`docker-compose.dev.yml`)
   235→
   236→Same as production-like, plus:
   237→
   238→| Variable | Default | Description |
   239→|----------|---------|-------------|
   240→| `RAILS_ENV` | `development` | Rails environment |
   241→| `SANDCASTLE_HOST` | `localhost` | Host for URLs |
   242→
   243→## Architecture
   244→
   245→### Services
   246→
   247→- **traefik** — Reverse proxy (ports 8080:80, 8443:443)
   248→- **postgres** — PostgreSQL 18 database
   249→- **web** — Rails app (Puma server on port 80 inside container)
   250→- **worker** — Solid Queue background worker
   251→- **migrate** — One-shot migration runner (runs on startup)
   252→- **init-data** — One-shot data directory initializer
   253→- **init-traefik** — One-shot Traefik config initializer
   254→
   255→### Networks
   256→
   257→- **sandcastle-web** — External bridge network (must be created manually)
   258→- **default** — Default compose network for internal communication
   259→
   260→### Volumes
   261→
   262→| Volume | Purpose |
   263→|--------|---------|
   264→| `pgdata` | PostgreSQL data |
   265→| `sandcastle-data` | User homes, sandbox volumes, Tailscale state |
   266→| `traefik-data` | Traefik config and certificates |
   267→| `bundle-data` | Ruby gems (dev mode only) |
   268→| `node-modules` | Node packages (dev mode only) |
   269→
   270→### Ports
   271→
   272→- **8080** — HTTP (Traefik web entrypoint)
   273→- **8443** — HTTPS (Traefik websecure entrypoint)
   274→- **2201-2299** — SSH ports for sandboxes (mapped to host)
   275→
   276→## Troubleshooting
   277→
   278→### Containers Won't Start
   279→
   280→```bash
   281→# Check container logs
   282→docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml logs
   283→
   284→# Inspect failed container
   285→docker inspect sandcastle-web
   286→
   287→# Check for port conflicts
   288→lsof -i :8080
   289→lsof -i :8443
   290→```
   291→
   292→### Database Connection Errors
   293→
   294→```bash
   295→# Check if postgres is healthy
   296→docker inspect sandcastle_postgres_1 | grep Health -A 10
   297→
   298→# Check database logs
   299→docker logs sandcastle_postgres_1
   300→
   301→# Try connecting manually
   302→docker exec -it sandcastle_postgres_1 psql -U sandcastle -d sandcastle_production
   303→```
   304→
   305→### Docker Socket Permission Denied
   306→
   307→The web/worker containers need access to `/var/run/docker.sock`. On macOS this usually works, on Linux you may need to set `DOCKER_GID`:
   308→
   309→```bash
   310→# Find Docker socket GID
   311→stat -c '%g' /var/run/docker.sock  # Linux
   312→stat -f '%g' /var/run/docker.sock  # macOS
   313→
   314→# Set it in your environment
   315→export DOCKER_GID=988
   316→mise run deploy:local
   317→```
   318→
   319→### Traefik Not Routing
   320→
   321→```bash
   322→# Check if sandcastle-web network exists
   323→docker network ls | grep sandcastle-web
   324→
   325→# Create it if missing
   326→docker network create sandcastle-web
   327→
   328→# Restart Traefik
   329→docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml restart traefik
   330→
   331→# Check Traefik logs
   332→docker logs sandcastle_traefik_1
   333→```
   334→
   335→### Sandbox Containers Won't Start
   336→
   337→Sandboxes require the Sysbox runtime:
   338→
   339→```bash
   340→# Check if Sysbox is installed
   341→docker info | grep -i sysbox
   342→
   343→# List available runtimes
   344→docker info | grep -A 5 Runtimes
   345→
   346→# Check Sysbox status (Linux)
   347→systemctl status sysbox
   348→```
   349→
   350→### Reset Everything
   351→
   352→If things are completely broken:
   353→
   354→```bash
   355→# Stop everything
   356→mise run deploy:local:down
   357→mise run deploy:dev:down
   358→
   359→# Remove all Sandcastle containers
   360→docker ps -a | grep -E "sandcastle|sc-wetty|sc-ts" | awk '{print $1}' | xargs -r docker rm -f
   361→
   362→# Remove all volumes
   363→docker volume ls | grep sandcastle | awk '{print $2}' | xargs -r docker volume rm
   364→
   365→# Remove network
   366→docker network rm sandcastle-web
   367→
   368→# Recreate network
   369→docker network create sandcastle-web
   370→
   371→# Start fresh
   372→mise run deploy:local:reset
   373→```
   374→
   375→## Default Admin Credentials
   376→
   377→The migrate service creates a default admin user (see `docker-compose.local.yml` lines 169-172):
   378→
   379→- **Username:** `thies`
   380→- **Email:** `thieso@gmail.com`
   381→- **Password:** `tubu`
   382→- **SSH Key:** (set in compose file)
   383→
   384→These are defined in the `SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_*` environment variables.
   385→
   386→## Development Workflow
   387→
   388→When using `mise run deploy:dev`:
   389→
   390→1. Source code is mounted at `/rails` in the container
   391→2. Changes to `.rb`, `.erb`, `.css` files reload automatically
   392→3. Tailwind CSS recompiles on save
   393→4. Gems are persisted in a volume (faster than bind mount)
   394→5. Database data persists between restarts
   395→
   396→**Tips:**
   397→- Edit files on the host, changes reflect immediately in the container
   398→- If you add a gem, rebuild: `docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml up --build`
   399→- Database migrations: `docker exec -it sandcastle-web ./bin/rails db:migrate`
   400→- Run tests: `docker exec -it sandcastle-web ./bin/rails test`
   401→
   402→## CLI Development
   403→
   404→The Go CLI is in `vendor/sandcastle-cli/`. It's not part of the Docker Compose setup:
   405→
   406→```bash
   407→cd vendor/sandcastle-cli
   408→
   409→# Build
   410→make build
   411→
   412→# Test against local Sandcastle
   413→export SANDCASTLE_API_URL=https://localhost:8443
   414→export SANDCASTLE_API_TOKEN=sc_...  # Get from UI
   415→./sandcastle list
   416→```
   417→
   418→## Useful Docker Commands
   419→
   420→```bash
   421→# Restart a single service
   422→docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml restart web
   423→
   424→# Rebuild a single service
   425→docker compose -f docker-compose.local.yml up --build web
   426→
   427→# Execute a command in a container
   428→docker exec -it sandcastle-web bash
   429→
   430→# Copy files from container
   431→docker cp sandcastle-web:/rails/log/production.log ./
   432→
   433→# Copy files to container
   434→docker cp ./config.yml sandcastle-web:/rails/config/
   435→
   436→# Inspect container details
   437→docker inspect sandcastle-web
   438→
   439→# View resource usage
   440→docker stats
   441→
   442→# Prune unused images/volumes (be careful!)
   443→docker system prune -a
   444→```
   445→
   446→## File Locations
   447→
   448→### On Host
   449→
   450→- **Compose files:** `docker-compose.{local,dev}.yml`
   451→- **Mise config:** `mise.toml`
   452→- **Source code:** `.` (mounted in dev mode)
   453→
   454→### In Container
   455→
   456→- **Rails root:** `/rails`
   457→- **Data directory:** `/data`
   458→  - User homes: `/data/users/{username}/home`
   459→  - Sandbox volumes: `/data/sandboxes/{sandbox_name}/vol`
   460→  - Tailscale state: `/data/users/{username}/tailscale`
   461→  - WeTTY keys: `/data/wetty/{full_name}/`
   462→- **Traefik config:** `/data/traefik/dynamic/`
   463→- **Database:** `/var/lib/postgresql` (in postgres container)
   464→
   465→## Accessing Services
   466→
   467→| Service | URL | Notes |
   468→|---------|-----|-------|
   469→| Web UI (HTTPS) | https://sandcastle.local:8443 | Recommended, supports terminals |
   470→| Web UI (HTTP) | http://sandcastle.local:8080 | No TLS |
   471→| PostgreSQL | `localhost:5432` | Not exposed by default |
   472→| Traefik | — | No dashboard exposed |
   473→
   474→Add to `/etc/hosts` for `sandcastle.local`:
   475→```
   476→127.0.0.1 sandcastle.local
   477→```
   478→
   479→For localhost, use `https://localhost:8443`.
   480→

```
     1→FROM ubuntu:25.10
     2→
     3→ARG SANDCASTLE_VERSION=dev
     4→
     5→ENV DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
     6→
     7→# System tools
     8→RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \
     9→    openssh-server sudo curl git tmux vim neovim \
    10→    build-essential \
    11→    jq ripgrep fd-find htop wget unzip ca-certificates net-tools iputils-ping \
    12→    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
    13→
    14→# GUI tools: TigerVNC (Xvnc = virtual X server + VNC server combined), and browser
    15→# Xvnc sends the RFB banner immediately on connect, unlike x11vnc 0.9.17+ which waits
    16→# for client data first (breaking websockify's server-speaks-first expectation).
    17→RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \
    18→    tigervnc-standalone-server openbox xterm xfonts-base xfonts-100dpi xfonts-75dpi \
    19→    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
    20→
    21→# Google Chrome (amd64) or Chromium (arm64) — Google doesn't ship a Chrome deb for arm64
    22→ARG TARGETARCH
    23→RUN if [ "$TARGETARCH" = "amd64" ]; then \
    24→      apt-get update \
    25→      && wget -q https://dl.google.com/linux/direct/google-chrome-stable_current_amd64.deb \
    26→      && apt-get install -y ./google-chrome-stable_current_amd64.deb \
    27→      && rm google-chrome-stable_current_amd64.deb \
    28→      && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*; \
    29→    else \
    30→      apt-get update \
    31→      && apt-get install -y chromium \
    32→      && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*; \
    33→    fi
    34→
    35→# Prefer IPv4 to avoid slow/broken IPv6 connections
    36→RUN sed -i 's/#precedence ::ffff:0:0\/96  100/precedence ::ffff:0:0\/96  100/' /etc/gai.conf
    37→
    38→# Docker CLI + daemon (Sysbox makes this safe)
    39→RUN curl -fsSL https://get.docker.com | sh
    40→
    41→# Pin runc to 1.1.x — runc 1.2+ added a procfs safety check that fails
    42→# inside sysbox containers (sysbox-fs mounts /proc/sys as separate FUSE)
    43→RUN RUNC_VERSION="v1.1.15" \
    44→    && ARCH=$(dpkg --print-architecture) \
    45→    && curl -fsSL "https://github.com/opencontainers/runc/releases/download/${RUNC_VERSION}/runc.${ARCH}" \
    46→       -o /usr/bin/runc \
    47→    && chmod +x /usr/bin/runc
    48→
    49→# Docker Compose plugin
    50→RUN mkdir -p /usr/local/lib/docker/cli-plugins \
    51→    && curl -fsSL "https://github.com/docker/compose/releases/latest/download/docker-compose-linux-$(uname -m)" \
    52→       -o /usr/local/lib/docker/cli-plugins/docker-compose \
    53→    && chmod +x /usr/local/lib/docker/cli-plugins/docker-compose
    54→
    55→# Stage mise + Claude Code in /opt/sandcastle/bin (seeded to ~/.local/bin on first boot)
    56→RUN mkdir -p /opt/sandcastle/bin \
    57→    && curl https://mise.run | REDACTED sh \
    58→    && curl -fsSL https://claude.ai/install.sh | bash \
    59→    && cp -L /root/.local/bin/claude /opt/sandcastle/bin/claude \
    60→    && rm -rf /root/.local/share/claude /root/.local/bin/claude
    61→
    62→# Entire CLI — git observability for AI agent sessions (https://entire.io)
    63→RUN ARCH="$(dpkg --print-architecture)" \
    64→    && curl -fsSL "https://github.com/entireio/cli/releases/latest/download/entire_linux_${ARCH}.tar.gz" \
    65→       | tar -xz -C /usr/local/bin entire \
    66→    && chmod +x /usr/local/bin/entire
    67→
    68→# Shell config: PATH, mise activation, aliases
    69→RUN echo 'export PATH="$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH"' >> /etc/bash.bashrc \
    70→    && echo 'eval "$(mise activate bash)"' >> /etc/bash.bashrc \
    71→    && echo "alias yolo='claude --dangerously-skip-permissions'" >> /etc/bash.bashrc
    72→
    73→# Bake version into the image
    74→RUN echo "${SANDCASTLE_VERSION}" > /etc/sandcastle-version
    75→
    76→# SSH configuration (key-only auth) — use drop-in config for Ubuntu 25.10 compatibility
    77→RUN mkdir -p /var/run/sshd /etc/ssh/sshd_config.d \
    78→    && echo "PermitRootLogin no" > /etc/ssh/sshd_config.d/10-sandcastle.conf \
    79→    && echo "PubkeyAuthentication yes" >> /etc/ssh/sshd_config.d/10-sandcastle.conf \
    80→    && echo "PasswordAuthentication no" >> /etc/ssh/sshd_config.d/10-sandcastle.conf
    81→
    82→COPY entrypoint.sh /entrypoint.sh
    83→RUN chmod +x /entrypoint.sh
    84→
    85→COPY startchrome.sh /usr/local/bin/google-chrome
    86→RUN chmod +x /usr/local/bin/google-chrome \
    87→    && ln -s /usr/local/bin/google-chrome /usr/local/bin/startchrome
    88→
    89→COPY tmux.conf /etc/tmux.conf
    90→COPY gitconfig /etc/gitconfig
    91→
    92→LABEL org.opencontainers.image.source="https://github.com/thieso2/sandcastle"
    93→LABEL org.opencontainers.image.description="Sandcastle sandbox image — Ubuntu 25.10 with Docker-in-Docker, SSH, and dev tools"
    94→LABEL org.opencontainers.image.licenses="MIT"
    95→
    96→WORKDIR /workspace
    97→EXPOSE 22 5900
    98→
    99→ENTRYPOINT ["/entrypoint.sh"]
   100→

     1→#!/bin/bash
     2→set -e
     3→
     4→USERNAME="${SANDCASTLE_USER:-sandbox}"
     5→SSH_KEY="${SANDCASTLE_SSH_KEY:-}"
     6→
     7→# Create user if not exists
     8→if ! id "$USERNAME" &>/dev/null; then
     9→    useradd -m -s /bin/bash -G sudo,docker "$USERNAME"
    10→    echo "$USERNAME ALL=(ALL) NOPASSWD:ALL" >> /etc/sudoers.d/sandcastle
    11→    chmod 0440 /etc/sudoers.d/sandcastle
    12→fi
    13→
    14→# Set up SSH authorized keys (append if not already present, preserving
    15→# any WeTTY keys that may have been injected for other sandboxes sharing
    16→# this user's home directory via bind mount).
    17→if [ -n "$SSH_KEY" ]; then
    18→    SSH_DIR="/home/$USERNAME/.ssh"
    19→    mkdir -p "$SSH_DIR"
    20→    if [ -f "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys" ]; then
    21→        grep -qF "$SSH_KEY" "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys" || echo "$SSH_KEY" >> "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys"
    22→    else
    23→        echo "$SSH_KEY" > "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys"
    24→    fi
    25→    chmod 700 "$SSH_DIR"
    26→    chmod 600 "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys"
    27→    chown -R "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" "$SSH_DIR"
    28→fi
    29→
    30→# Ensure home directory ownership and permissions.
    31→# The host may create bind-mounted home dirs with 777 so Sysbox-mapped root
    32→# can write. Tighten to 755 here so sshd StrictModes is satisfied.
    33→chown "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" "/home/$USERNAME"
    34→chmod 755 "/home/$USERNAME"
    35→
    36→# Ensure workspace is accessible
    37→chown "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" /workspace 2>/dev/null || true
    38→
    39→# Seed mise + Claude Code into user's ~/.local/bin on first boot
    40→USER_LOCAL_BIN="/home/$USERNAME/.local/bin"
    41→mkdir -p "$USER_LOCAL_BIN"
    42→for tool in mise claude; do
    43→    if [ ! -f "$USER_LOCAL_BIN/$tool" ] && [ -f "/opt/sandcastle/bin/$tool" ]; then
    44→        cp "/opt/sandcastle/bin/$tool" "$USER_LOCAL_BIN/$tool"
    45→    fi
    46→done
    47→chown -R "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" "/home/$USERNAME/.local"
    48→
    49→# Configure git identity system-wide if provided
    50→if [ -n "$USER_FULLNAME" ] || [ -n "$USER_EMAIL" ]; then
    51→    {
    52→        echo "[user]"
    53→        [ -n "$USER_FULLNAME" ] && echo "    name = $USER_FULLNAME"
    54→        [ -n "$USER_EMAIL" ] && echo "    email = $USER_EMAIL"
    55→    } >> /etc/gitconfig
    56→fi
    57→
    58→# Generate SSH host keys if missing
    59→ssh-keygen -A
    60→
    61→# Resize /dev/shm to 2GB for Chrome. Docker's ShmSize HostConfig key is not
    62→# supported by sysbox-runc, so we do it here instead. The runtime bind-mounts
    63→# /dev/shm from a small constrained shm, so "remount,size=" fails; unmounting
    64→# it and mounting a fresh tmpfs works.
    65→umount /dev/shm 2>/dev/null || true
    66→mount -t tmpfs -o size=2g,mode=1777 tmpfs /dev/shm 2>/dev/null || true
    67→
    68→# Start Docker daemon in background (requires Sysbox runtime for isolated /var/lib/docker)
    69→# Don't wait for it to be ready - users can check with `docker info` after SSH login
    70→if command -v dockerd &>/dev/null && [ -e /dev/fuse ]; then
    71→    # Match inner Docker bridge MTU to container's eth0 to avoid packet fragmentation
    72→    ETH0_MTU=$(ip link show eth0 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'mtu \K[0-9]+' || echo 1500)
    73→    dockerd --storage-driver=overlay2 --mtu="$ETH0_MTU" &>/var/log/dockerd.log &
    74→else
    75→    echo "Note: Docker-in-Docker not available (requires sysbox-runc runtime)" >&2
    76→fi
    77→
    78→# Start virtual X + VNC server for browser access.
    79→# Xvnc (TigerVNC) combines Xvfb and a VNC server in a single process and sends
    80→# the RFB banner immediately on connect — required for websockify compatibility.
    81→# x11vnc 0.9.17+ waits for client data before sending the banner, deadlocking
    82→# with websockify which also waits for the server to speak first.
    83→# Both processes run as $USERNAME (not root) for proper display ownership.
    84→VNC_ENABLED="${SANDCASTLE_VNC_ENABLED:-1}"
    85→VNC_GEOMETRY="${SANDCASTLE_VNC_GEOMETRY:-1280x900}"
    86→VNC_DEPTH="${SANDCASTLE_VNC_DEPTH:-24}"
    87→
    88→if command -v Xvnc &>/dev/null && [ "$VNC_ENABLED" = "1" ]; then
    89→    touch /var/log/xvnc.log /var/log/openbox.log
    90→    chown "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" /var/log/xvnc.log /var/log/openbox.log
    91→    su -s /bin/bash "$USERNAME" -c \
    92→        "Xvnc :99 -rfbport 5900 -SecurityTypes None -AlwaysShared -geometry ${VNC_GEOMETRY} -depth ${VNC_DEPTH} &>/var/log/xvnc.log &"
    93→    # Start Openbox window manager once the display is ready
    94→    if command -v openbox &>/dev/null; then
    95→        su -s /bin/bash "$USERNAME" -c \
    96→            'DISPLAY=:99 openbox &>/var/log/openbox.log &'
    97→    fi
    98→fi
    99→
   100→# Start SSH daemon in foreground
   101→exec /usr/sbin/sshd -D -e
   102→

> AGENT

**Root cause:** The sandbox image on the registry predates the VNC commits. `Xvnc` was added in recent commits but no release has been tagged yet, so the pulled image is still the old one.

**Fix:** Build the sandbox image locally and recreate the sandboxes.

```bash
# Build sandbox image locally
docker build -t ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest images/sandbox/
```

Then in the UI, destroy `thies-bold-orca` (and `thies-digital-tiger` if it still exists) and recreate them — the new containers will pull and use the freshly built image.

Alternatively, do a release to push the new image to the registry:

```bash
mise run release
```

That will tag + push, trigger CI to build the sandbox image with VNC, and after CI completes the registry image will have `Xvnc` included.

```
     1→class DashboardController < ApplicationController
     2→  def index
     3→    @sandboxes = policy_scope(Sandbox).includes(:user, :routes).order(:name)
     4→  end
     5→
     6→  def card
     7→    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).includes(:user, :routes).find(params[:id])
     8→    authorize sandbox
     9→    render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(helpers.dom_id(sandbox), partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: sandbox })
    10→  end
    11→
    12→  def stats
    13→    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])
    14→    authorize sandbox, :stats?
    15→
    16→    if sandbox.status == "running" && sandbox.container_id.present?
    17→      container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
    18→      raw = container.stats(stream: false)
    19→
    20→      # Handle case where container is shutting down and returns nil/incomplete stats
    21→      if raw.present?
    22→        networks = raw["networks"] || {}
    23→        net_rx = networks.values.sum { |n| n["rx_bytes"] || 0 }
    24→        net_tx = networks.values.sum { |n| n["tx_bytes"] || 0 }
    25→
    26→        blkio = raw.dig("blkio_stats", "io_service_bytes_recursive") || []
    27→        disk_read = blkio.select { |e| e["op"]&.downcase == "read" }.sum { |e| e["value"] || 0 }
    28→        disk_write = blkio.select { |e| e["op"]&.downcase == "write" }.sum { |e| e["value"] || 0 }
    29→
    30→        @stats = {
    31→          cpu_percent: calculate_cpu_percent(raw),
    32→          memory_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "usage") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
    33→          memory_limit_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "limit") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
    34→          net_rx: net_rx,
    35→          net_tx: net_tx,
    36→          disk_read: disk_read,
    37→          disk_write: disk_write,
    38→          pids: raw.dig("pids_stats", "current") || 0
    39→        }
    40→      end
    41→    end
    42→
    43→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: @stats, sandbox: sandbox }
    44→  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound
    45→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: nil, sandbox: nil }
    46→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
    47→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: nil, sandbox: sandbox }
    48→  end
    49→
    50→  private
    51→
    52→  def calculate_cpu_percent(stats)
    53→    cpu_delta = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "cpu_usage", "total_usage").to_f -
    54→                stats.dig("precpu_stats", "cpu_usage", "total_usage").to_f
    55→    system_delta = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "system_cpu_usage").to_f -
    56→                   stats.dig("precpu_stats", "system_cpu_usage").to_f
    57→    num_cpus = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "online_cpus") || 1
    58→
    59→    return 0.0 if system_delta.zero?
    60→    ((cpu_delta / system_delta) * num_cpus * 100.0).round(1)
    61→  end
    62→end
    63→

```
     1→class DashboardController < ApplicationController
     2→  def index
     3→    @sandboxes = policy_scope(Sandbox).includes(:user, :routes).order(:name)
     4→    @vnc_active_names = running_vnc_names
     5→  end
     6→
     7→  def card
     8→    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).includes(:user, :routes).find(params[:id])
     9→    authorize sandbox
    10→    vnc_active = VncManager.new.active?(sandbox: sandbox)
    11→    render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(helpers.dom_id(sandbox), partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: sandbox, vnc_active: vnc_active })
    12→  end
    13→
    14→  def stats
    15→    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])
    16→    authorize sandbox, :stats?
    17→
    18→    if sandbox.status == "running" && sandbox.container_id.present?
    19→      container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
    20→      raw = container.stats(stream: false)
    21→
    22→      # Handle case where container is shutting down and returns nil/incomplete stats
    23→      if raw.present?
    24→        networks = raw["networks"] || {}
    25→        net_rx = networks.values.sum { |n| n["rx_bytes"] || 0 }
    26→        net_tx = networks.values.sum { |n| n["tx_bytes"] || 0 }
    27→
    28→        blkio = raw.dig("blkio_stats", "io_service_bytes_recursive") || []
    29→        disk_read = blkio.select { |e| e["op"]&.downcase == "read" }.sum { |e| e["value"] || 0 }
    30→        disk_write = blkio.select { |e| e["op"]&.downcase == "write" }.sum { |e| e["value"] || 0 }
    31→
    32→        @stats = {
    33→          cpu_percent: calculate_cpu_percent(raw),
    34→          memory_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "usage") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
    35→          memory_limit_mb: (raw.dig("memory_stats", "limit") || 0) / 1_048_576.0,
    36→          net_rx: net_rx,
    37→          net_tx: net_tx,
    38→          disk_read: disk_read,
    39→          disk_write: disk_write,
    40→          pids: raw.dig("pids_stats", "current") || 0
    41→        }
    42→      end
    43→    end
    44→
    45→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: @stats, sandbox: sandbox }
    46→  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound
    47→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: nil, sandbox: nil }
    48→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
    49→    render partial: "sandbox_stats", locals: { stats: nil, sandbox: sandbox }
    50→  end
    51→
    52→  private
    53→
    54→  def running_vnc_names
    55→    Docker::Container.all.filter_map { |c|
    56→      name = c.info.dig("Names")&.first&.delete_prefix("/")
    57→      name.delete_prefix("sc-vnc-") if name&.start_with?("sc-vnc-")
    58→    }.to_set
    59→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
    60→    Set.new
    61→  end
    62→
    63→  def calculate_cpu_percent(stats)
    64→    cpu_delta = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "cpu_usage", "total_usage").to_f -
    65→                stats.dig("precpu_stats", "cpu_usage", "total_usage").to_f
    66→    system_delta = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "system_cpu_usage").to_f -
    67→                   stats.dig("precpu_stats", "system_cpu_usage").to_f
    68→    num_cpus = stats.dig("cpu_stats", "online_cpus") || 1
    69→
    70→    return 0.0 if system_delta.zero?
    71→    ((cpu_delta / system_delta) * num_cpus * 100.0).round(1)
    72→  end
    73→end
    74→

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.

> AGENT

Done. Here's what was added:

- **"browser" pill** (indigo, with monitor icon) appears in the sandbox card's pill row whenever an active noVNC container is detected for that sandbox
- **"Close" button** appears next to the "Browser" button when a session is active — sends `DELETE /sandboxes/:id/vnc` to tear it down
- **Dashboard index** does a single `Docker::Container.all` call and filters `sc-vnc-*` names into a Set — no per-sandbox Docker round trips
- **Turbo Stream card refresh** (`card` action) calls `VncManager#active?` for the individual sandbox so the status stays correct after partial re-renders

id: toolu_01Cnrjc5B5hFco4YDSxHJD4q
```
title:	feat(snapshots): implement full composite snapshot management
state:	OPEN
author:	thieso2
labels:	
assignees:	
reviewers:	
projects:	
milestone:	
number:	38
url:	https://github.com/thieso2/Sandcastle/pull/38
additions:	1277
deletions:	125
auto-merge:	disabled
--
Adds a unified snapshot system that combines Docker container images with optional BTRFS copy-on-write snapshots of home and data directories.

Key changes:
- DB: new `snapshots` table with per-layer fields (docker_image, home_snapshot, data_snapshot) and size tracking
- Model: Snapshot AR model with layers() and total_size() helpers
- Policy: SnapshotPolicy (Pundit) for owner/admin authorization
- BtrfsHelper: snapshot_subvolume, delete_snapshot, subvolume_size, restore_subvolume methods for read-only BTRFS snapshot lifecycle
- SandboxManager: create_snapshot (composite), list_snapshots (DB + legacy import), destroy_snapshot (all layers), restore (per-layer support), import_legacy_snapshots (idempotent Docker→DB migration)
- API: POST /api/snapshots (create), GET/DELETE /api/snapshots/:name (show/destroy) with layers and data_subdir params; sandbox restore endpoint supports layers
- Web: sandbox show page with snapshot list + create modal; global /snapshots page; navbar link; sandbox create form uses richer snapshot labels
- CLI: `sandcastle snapshot create/list/show/destroy/restore` with --label, --layers, --data-subdir, --restore-layers flags; `sandbox create --from-snapshot`
- Installer: ensures /data/snapshots directory is created and owned
- Guide: updated snapshot CLI documentation with full examples

Backward compatible: existing Docker-only snapshots are auto-imported as DB records; legacy `snapshot` API method preserved as alias.
```

    77→  SANDCASTLE_DATA=.    equivalent to --data (value is the subpath, "." or "1" for root)
    78→  SANDCASTLE_RM=1      equivalent to --rm
    79→
    80→Flags explicitly passed on the command line take precedence over environment variables.`,

   228→
   229→		if err := waitForSSH(info.Host, info.Port); err != nil {
   230→			return err
   231→		}
   232→
   233→		sshErr := sshExec(info.Host, info.Port, info.User, "tmux new-session -A -s main")
   234→
   235→		if sandboxRemove {
   236→			// Re-fetch to check if user toggled to "keep" during the session
   237→			current, fetchErr := client.GetSandbox(sandbox.ID)
   238→			if fetchErr == nil && !current.Temporary {
   239→				fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q was set to keep — skipping removal.\n", sandbox.Name)
   240→			} else {
   241→				fmt.Printf("Removing sandbox %q...\n", sandbox.Name)
   242→				if err := client.DestroySandbox(sandbox.ID); err != nil {
   243→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to delete sandbox: %v\n", err)
   244→				} else {
   245→					fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q deleted.\n", sandbox.Name)
   246→				}
   247→			}
   248→		}
   249→
   250→		return sshErr
   251→	},
   252→}
   253→
   254→var listCmd = &cobra.Command{
   255→	Use:     "list",
   256→	Aliases: []string{"ls"},
   257→	Short:   "List all sandboxes",
   258→	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   259→		client, err := api.NewClient()
   260→		if err != nil {
   261→			return err
   262→		}
   263→		printServer(client)
   264→
   265→		sandboxes, err := client.ListSandboxes()
   266→		if err != nil {
   267→			return err
   268→		}
   269→
   270→		if len(sandboxes) == 0 {
   271→			fmt.Println("No sandboxes.")
   272→			return nil
   273→		}
   274→
   275→		hasRoute := false
   276→		for _, s := range sandboxes {
   277→			if len(s.Routes) > 0 {
   278→				hasRoute = true
   279→				break
   280→			}
   281→		}
   282→
   283→		w := tabwriter.NewWriter(os.Stdout, 0, 0, 2, ' ', 0)
   284→		if hasRoute {
   285→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "NAME\tSTATUS\tPORT\tROUTE\tTAILSCALE IP\tIMAGE")
   286→		} else {
   287→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "NAME\tSTATUS\tPORT\tTAILSCALE IP\tIMAGE")
   288→		}
   289→		for _, s := range sandboxes {
   290→			name := s.Name
   291→			if s.Temporary {
   292→				name += " (temp)"
   293→			}
   294→			tsIP := ""
   295→			if s.TailscaleIP != "" {
   296→				tsIP = s.TailscaleIP
   297→			}
   298→			if hasRoute {
   299→				route := ""
   300→				if len(s.Routes) > 0 {
   301→					parts := make([]string, len(s.Routes))
   302→					for i, r := range s.Routes {
   303→						parts[i] = fmt.Sprintf("%s (:%d)", r.URL, r.Port)
   304→					}
   305→					route = strings.Join(parts, ", ")
   306→				}
   307→				fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\t%s\t%d\t%s\t%s\t%s\n", name, s.Status, s.SSHPort, route, tsIP, s.Image)
   308→			} else {
   309→				fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\t%s\t%d\t%s\t%s\n", name, s.Status, s.SSHPort, tsIP, s.Image)
   310→			}
   311→		}
   312→		w.Flush()
   313→		return nil
   314→	},
   315→}

   394→var useCmd = &cobra.Command{
   395→	Use:   "use [name]",
   396→	Short: "Show or set active server/sandbox",
   397→	Long: `Without arguments, shows the current server and active sandbox.
   398→With an argument, switches the active server or sandbox.
   399→
   400→Examples:
   401→  sandcastle use                  # Show current server and sandbox
   402→  sandcastle use my-sandbox       # Set active sandbox
   403→  sandcastle use prod             # Switch to server "prod" (if configured)`,
   404→	Args: cobra.MaximumNArgs(1),
   405→	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   406→		cfg, err := config.Load()
   407→		if err != nil {
   408→			return err
   409→		}
   410→
   411→		// No args: list all servers, highlight active
   412→		if len(args) == 0 {
   413→			if len(cfg.Servers) == 0 {
   414→				fmt.Println("No servers configured — run: sandcastle login <url>")
   415→				return nil
   416→			}
   417→
   418→			aliases := make([]string, 0, len(cfg.Servers))
   419→			for alias := range cfg.Servers {
   420→				aliases = append(aliases, alias)
   421→			}
   422→			sort.Strings(aliases)
   423→
   424→			for _, alias := range aliases {
   425→				srv := cfg.Servers[alias]
   426→				if alias == cfg.CurrentServer {
   427→					fmt.Printf("  \033[1m* %-16s\033[0m %s\n", alias, srv.URL)
   428→				} else {
   429→					fmt.Printf("    %-16s %s\n", alias, srv.URL)
   430→				}
   431→			}
   432→
   433→			return nil
   434→		}
   435→
   436→		name := args[0]
   437→
   438→		// Check if name matches a server alias or URL
   439→		if _, ok := cfg.Servers[name]; ok {
   440→			cfg.CurrentServer = name
   441→			if err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {
   442→				return err
   443→			}
   444→			fmt.Printf("Switched to server %s (%s)\n", name, cfg.Servers[name].URL)
   445→			return nil
   446→		}
   447→
   448→		// Try matching by URL
   449→		for alias, srv := range cfg.Servers {
   450→			if srv.URL == strings.TrimRight(name, "/") {
   451→				cfg.CurrentServer = alias
   452→				if err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {
   453→					return err
   454→				}
   455→				fmt.Printf("Switched to server %s (%s)\n", alias, srv.URL)
   456→				return nil
   457→			}
   458→		}
   459→
   460→		return fmt.Errorf("server %q not found — run: sandcastle use", name)
   461→	},
   462→}
   463→
   464→var setCmd = &cobra.Command{
   465→	Use:   "set <name> <temp|keep>",
   466→	Short: "Toggle sandbox between temporary and kept",
   467→	Long: `Toggle a sandbox between temporary (auto-remove on exit) and kept.
   468→
   469→  temp   Mark as temporary — will be removed when the CLI session exits
   470→  keep   Mark as kept — will not be auto-removed`,
   471→	Args: cobra.ExactArgs(2),
   472→	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   473→		client, err := api.NewClient()
   474→		if err != nil {
   475→			return err
   476→		}
   477→		printServer(client)
   478→
   479→		sandbox, err := findSandboxByName(client, args[0])
   480→		if err != nil {
   481→			return err
   482→		}
   483→
   484→		mode := strings.ToLower(args[1])
   485→		var temp bool
   486→		switch mode {
   487→		case "temp":
   488→			temp = true
   489→		case "keep":
   490→			temp = false
   491→		default:
   492→			return fmt.Errorf("unknown mode %q: use \"temp\" or \"keep\"", args[1])
   493→		}
   494→
   495→		sandbox, err = client.UpdateSandbox(sandbox.ID, api.UpdateSandboxRequest{Temporary: &temp})
   496→		if err != nil {
   497→			return err
   498→		}
   499→
   500→		if temp {
   501→			fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q set to temporary (will be removed on exit).\n", sandbox.Name)
   502→		} else {
   503→			fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q set to keep (will not be removed on exit).\n", sandbox.Name)
   504→		}
   505→		return nil
   506→	},
   507→}
   508→
   509→func envTruthy(key string) bool {
   510→	v := strings.ToLower(os.Getenv(key))
   511→	return v == "1" || v == "true" || v == "yes"
   512→}
   513→
   514→func findSandboxByName(client *api.Client, name string) (*api.Sandbox, error) {
   515→	sandboxes, err := client.ListSandboxes()
   516→	if err != nil {
   517→		return nil, err
   518→	}
   519→	for _, s := range sandboxes {
   520→		if s.Name == name {
   521→			return &s, nil
   522→		}
   523→	}
   524→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("sandbox %q not found", name)
   525→}

Perfect! I've successfully read all the requested files. Here's a summary of what I found:

## File Contents Summary

### 1. **app/views/shared/_navbar.html.erb** (55 lines)
Navigation bar with logo, links to Dashboard, Snapshots, Guide, Tailscale, Admin (if admin), and Jobs. Also displays user name, settings link, and logout button.

### 2. **app/views/sandboxes/show.html.erb** (159 lines)
Sandbox detail page showing:
- Sandbox name and status badge
- SSH port information
- Snapshots tab with a table listing all snapshots for the current sandbox
- Create Snapshot modal with:
  - Snapshot name field (required, with pattern validation)
  - Optional description/label
  - Layer selection checkboxes (container, home, data, workspace)
  - BTRFS availability indicator
- Clone and Delete buttons for each snapshot

### 3. **app/views/sandboxes/new.html.erb** (190 lines)
Create sandbox form with sections for:
- Basic Information (name, image)
- Restore from Snapshot (if snapshots exist)
- Storage Options (persistent volume, mount home, data path)
- Network Options (Tailscale, if user has it enabled)
- VNC/Display Options (enable/disable, resolution, color depth)
- JavaScript for auto-generating sandbox names and toggling VNC options

### 4. **app/controllers/sandboxes_controller.rb** (132 lines)
Sandbox controller with actions:
- `new` - lists available snapshots and checks BTRFS availability
- `show` - filters snapshots by source sandbox
- `create` - creates sandbox from form params (with snapshot support)
- `destroy` - enqueues destruction job
- `start` - enqueues start job
- `stop` - enqueues stop job
- `retry` - retries failed operations

### 5. **app/controllers/snapshots_controller.rb** (53 lines)
Snapshots controller with actions:
- `index` - lists user snapshots
- `create_for_sandbox` - creates snapshot for a specific sandbox
- `destroy` - deletes snapshot
- `clone` - redirects to new sandbox form with snapshot pre-filled
- Legacy snapshot import on `set_snapshot` if not found in DB

### 6. **app/models/snapshot.rb** (21 lines)
Snapshot ActiveRecord model with:
- Belongs to User
- Name validation (lowercase alphanumeric, hyphens, underscores)
- `layers` method - returns array of available layers (container, home, data)
- `total_size` - sums docker_size, home_size, data_size

### 7. **config/routes.rb** (119 lines)
Routes configuration with:
- Sandboxes resource with member routes: start, stop, retry, stats, card, terminal (open/wait/status/close), vnc (open/wait/status/close), snapshot (create)
- Snapshots resource with index/destroy and clone member route
- API namespace with snapshots CRUD, sandbox restore, snapshot restore
- Tailscale routes
- Admin namespace with dashboard, users, sandboxes management, jobs

### 8. **app/services/sandbox_manager.rb** (598 lines)
Core sandbox management service with snapshot-related methods:

**Snapshot Methods:**
- **`create_snapshot(sandbox:, name:, label:, layers:, data_subdir:)`** - Creates composite snapshots with:
  - Container layer (docker commit)
  - Home layer (BTRFS snapshot)
  - Data layer (BTRFS snapshot)
  - Workspace layer (BTRFS snapshot)
  - Saves Snapshot DB record with all metadata

- **`list_snapshots(user:)`** - Lists all user snapshots (imports legacy ones first)

- **`destroy_snapshot(user:, name:)`** - Deletes snapshot:
  - Removes Docker image
  - Removes BTRFS snapshots
  - Destroys Snapshot record

- **`restore(sandbox:, snapshot_name:, layers:)`** - Restores snapshot:
  - Cleans up terminal/VNC/Tailscale
  - Stops/removes old container
  - Restores home directory (BTRFS)
  - Restores data directory (BTRFS)
  - Recreates container with snapshot image
  - Reconnects Tailscale if applicable

**Helper Methods:**
- `snapshot_json` - Serializes Snapshot record to JSON
- `import_legacy_snapshots` - Imports legacy Docker-only snapshots as DB records

All files are properly structured and use Rails conventions with Tailwind CSS for styling.

    68→              <td class="px-6 py-3 text-right">
    69→                <div class="flex items-center gap-2 justify-end">

    77→                        class: "text-red-600 hover:text-red-800 text-xs bg-transparent border-0 cursor-pointer p-0" %>
    78→                </div>
    79→              </td>
    80→            </tr>
    81→          <% end %>
    82→        </tbody>

    84→    <% end %>

     1→class SnapshotsController < ApplicationController
     2→  before_action :set_snapshot, only: %i[destroy clone]
     3→
     4→  def index
     5→    authorize Snapshot
     6→    @snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
     7→  end
     8→
     9→  # POST /sandboxes/:id/snapshot
    10→  def create_for_sandbox
    11→    # Member route provides sandbox id as params[:id]
    12→    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])
    13→    authorize sandbox, :snapshot?
    14→
    15→    layers = params[:layers].present? ? Array(params[:layers]) : nil
    16→
    17→    SandboxManager.new.create_snapshot(
    18→      sandbox: sandbox,
    19→      name: params.require(:name),
    20→      label: params[:label].presence,
    21→      layers: layers,
    22→      data_subdir: params[:data_subdir].presence
    23→    )
    24→
    25→    redirect_to sandbox_path(sandbox), notice: "Snapshot created."
    26→  rescue SandboxManager::Error => e
    27→    redirect_back_or_to root_path, alert: "Failed to create snapshot: #{e.message}"
    28→  end
    29→
    30→  def destroy
    31→    SandboxManager.new.destroy_snapshot(user: Current.user, name: params[:name])
    32→    redirect_to snapshots_path, notice: "Snapshot deleted."
    33→  rescue SandboxManager::Error => e
    34→    redirect_to snapshots_path, alert: "Failed to delete snapshot: #{e.message}"
    35→  end
    36→
    37→  # POST /snapshots/:name/clone — redirect to new sandbox form pre-filled with snapshot
    38→  def clone
    39→    authorize @snapshot, :show?
    40→    redirect_to new_sandbox_path(snapshot: @snapshot.name)
    41→  end
    42→
    43→  private
    44→
    45→  def set_snapshot
    46→    @snapshot = Snapshot.find_by!(user: Current.user, name: params[:name])
    47→    authorize @snapshot
    48→  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound
    49→    SandboxManager.new.import_legacy_snapshots(Current.user)
    50→    @snapshot = Snapshot.find_by!(user: Current.user, name: params[:name])
    51→    authorize @snapshot
    52→  end
    53→end
    54→

     1→class SandboxesController < ApplicationController
     2→  before_action :set_sandbox, only: [ :show, :destroy, :start, :stop, :retry ]
     3→
     4→  def new
     5→    authorize Sandbox
     6→    @snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
     7→    @btrfs_available = BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
     8→  end
     9→
    10→  def show
    11→    @sandbox_snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
    12→                           .select { |s| s[:source_sandbox] == @sandbox.name }
    13→    @btrfs = BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
    14→  end
    15→
    16→  def create
    17→    authorize Sandbox
    18→
    19→    from_snapshot_name = params[:snapshot].presence
    20→
    21→    image = if from_snapshot_name.present?
    22→      snap = Snapshot.find_by(user: Current.user, name: from_snapshot_name)
    23→      snap&.docker_image || "sc-snap-#{Current.user.name}:#{from_snapshot_name}"
    24→    else
    25→      params[:image].presence || SandboxManager::DEFAULT_IMAGE
    26→    end
    27→
    28→    # Build sandbox record
    29→    # Note: temporary sandboxes can only be created via CLI
    30→    sandbox = Current.user.sandboxes.build(
    31→      name: params.require(:name),
    32→      status: "pending",
    33→      image: image,
    34→      persistent_volume: params[:persistent] == "1",
    35→      mount_home: params[:mount_home] == "1",
    36→      data_path: params[:data_path].presence,
    37→      tailscale: params[:tailscale] == "1",
    38→      vnc_enabled: params[:vnc_enabled] != "0",
    39→      vnc_geometry: Sandbox::VNC_GEOMETRIES.include?(params[:vnc_geometry]) ? params[:vnc_geometry] : "1280x900",
    40→      vnc_depth: Sandbox::VNC_DEPTHS.include?(params[:vnc_depth].to_i) ? params[:vnc_depth].to_i : 24,
    41→      temporary: false
    42→    )
    43→
    44→    if sandbox.persistent_volume
    45→      sandbox.volume_path = "#{SandboxManager::DATA_DIR}/sandboxes/#{sandbox.full_name}/vol"
    46→    end
    47→
    48→    if sandbox.save
    49→      # Enqueue async job
    50→      SandboxProvisionJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: sandbox.id)
    51→
    52→      # Optimistic redirect - dashboard will update via Turbo
    53→      redirect_to root_path, notice: "Creating sandcastle #{sandbox.name}..."
    54→    else
    55→      @snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
    56→      flash.now[:alert] = "Failed to create sandbox: #{sandbox.errors.full_messages.join(', ')}"
    57→      render :new, status: :unprocessable_entity
    58→    end
    59→  rescue => e
    60→    @snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
    61→    flash.now[:alert] = "Failed to create sandbox: #{e.message}"
    62→    render :new, status: :unprocessable_entity
    63→  end
    64→
    65→  def destroy
    66→    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
    67→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
    68→      return
    69→    end
    70→
    71→    @sandbox.start_job("destroying")
    72→    SandboxDestroyJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
    73→
    74→    respond_to do |format|
    75→      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: "Destroying sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." }
    76→      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
    77→    end
    78→  end
    79→
    80→  def start
    81→    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
    82→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
    83→      return
    84→    end
    85→
    86→    @sandbox.start_job("starting")
    87→    SandboxStartJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
    88→
    89→    respond_to do |format|
    90→      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: "Starting sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." }
    91→      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
    92→    end
    93→  end
    94→
    95→  def stop
    96→    if @sandbox.job_in_progress?
    97→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Operation already in progress"
    98→      return
    99→    end
   100→
   101→    @sandbox.start_job("stopping")
   102→    SandboxStopJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
   103→
   104→    respond_to do |format|
   105→      format.html { redirect_to root_path, notice: "Stopping sandcastle #{@sandbox.name}..." }
   106→      format.turbo_stream { render turbo_stream: turbo_stream.replace(@sandbox, partial: "dashboard/sandbox", locals: { sandbox: @sandbox }) }
   107→    end
   108→  end
   109→
   110→  def retry
   111→    return unless @sandbox.job_failed?
   112→
   113→    @sandbox.update!(job_error: nil)
   114→
   115→    case @sandbox.status
   116→    when "destroyed", "pending"
   117→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Cannot retry creation. Please create a new sandbox."
   118→    when "stopped"
   119→      SandboxStartJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: @sandbox.id)
   120→      redirect_to root_path, notice: "Retrying start..."
   121→    when "running"
   122→      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Sandbox is already running"
   123→    end
   124→  end
   125→
   126→  private
   127→
   128→  def set_sandbox
   129→    @sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])
   130→    authorize @sandbox
   131→  end
   132→end
   133→

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.
</system-reminder>

> AGENT

Done. Here's what changed:

**Dashboard card:**
- Sandbox name is now a clickable link → detail page
- Camera "Snapshot" button (running sandboxes) opens a lightweight modal right on the card (name + description, uses default layers)

**Sandbox detail page (`/sandboxes/:id`):**
- Action bar: Terminal, Browser, Snapshot, Stop/Start, Destroy
- Snapshot list with "New sandbox" (pre-fills the create form) and Delete per row
- Full create snapshot modal with layer selection (container/home/data/workspace depending on BTRFS)

**Sandbox create form:**
- Snapshot dropdown now shows `name — description [layers] (from sandbox)` for richer context

**Removed:**
- "Snapshots" link from the navbar
- `SnapshotsController#destroy` no longer redirects to the gone snapshots page — uses `redirect_back_or_to root_path` instead

> AGENT

The problem: `j` returns a plain String (not html_safe) when given a non-html_safe input, so `<%= %>` then HTML-escapes the result, turning `&` into `&amp;`. Fix — use `.to_json` which is always html_safe:

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_015eYFo3o8Cg8fNMagZLWwJ8
```json
{
  "command": "cd /Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle && git diff app/views/vnc/wait.html.erb",
  "description": "Check VNC wait view diff"
}
```

index 8dde470..282a699 100644
--- a/app/views/vnc/wait.html.erb
+++ b/app/views/vnc/wait.html.erb
@@ -31,7 +31,7 @@
 <script>
   (function() {
     var statusUrl = "<%= vnc_status_sandbox_path(@sandbox) %>";
-    var vncUrl = "<%= j @vnc_url %>";
+    var vncUrl = <%= @vnc_url.to_json %>;
     var elapsed = 0;
     var timeout = 30;

{
  "command": "git add app/views/vnc/wait.html.erb && git commit -m \"fix(vnc): use .to_json to prevent &amp; encoding in JS string\n\nUsing 'j @vnc_url' in a quoted JS string causes Rails to HTML-escape '&'\nto '&amp;', breaking the autoconnect=true URL parameter. .to_json returns\nan html_safe JSON string that doesn't get double-escaped.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>\" -- app/views/vnc/wait.html.erb && git push",
  "description": "Commit and push VNC autoconnect fix"
}
```

     1→class SandboxProvisionJob < ApplicationJob
     2→  queue_as :default
     3→
     4→  def perform(sandbox_id:)
     5→    sandbox = Sandbox.find(sandbox_id)
     6→    return if sandbox.status == "running" # Idempotent
     7→
     8→    sandbox.start_job("creating")
     9→    manager = SandboxManager.new
    10→
    11→    begin
    12→      # Directory creation
    13→      manager.ensure_mount_dirs(sandbox.user, sandbox)
    14→
    15→      # Image pull (can be slow)
    16→      manager.ensure_image(sandbox.image)
    17→
    18→      # Container creation and start
    19→      manager.create_container_and_start(sandbox: sandbox, user: sandbox.user)
    20→
    21→      # Connect to Tailscale if enabled
    22→      if sandbox.tailscale? && sandbox.user.tailscale_enabled?
    23→        TailscaleManager.new.connect_sandbox(sandbox: sandbox)
    24→      end
    25→
    26→      sandbox.update!(status: "running")
    27→      sandbox.finish_job
    28→
    29→    rescue => e
    30→      Rails.logger.error("SandboxProvisionJob failed: #{e.message}\n#{e.backtrace.join("\n")}")
    31→      sandbox.fail_job("Failed to create: #{e.message}")
    32→      sandbox.update!(status: "destroyed")
    33→
    34→      raise # Re-raise for Solid Queue retry
    35→    end
    36→  end
    37→end
    38→

     1→class Sandbox < ApplicationRecord
     2→  include ActionView::RecordIdentifier  # For dom_id in Turbo broadcasts
     3→
     4→  belongs_to :user
     5→  has_many :routes, dependent: :destroy
     6→
     7→  SSH_PORT_RANGE = (2201..2299)
     8→  VNC_GEOMETRIES = %w[1280x900 1366x768 1440x900 1600x900 1920x1080 2560x1440].freeze
     9→  VNC_DEPTHS = [ 8, 16, 24, 32 ].freeze
    10→
    11→  validates :name, presence: true,
    12→    uniqueness: { scope: :user_id, conditions: -> { where.not(status: "destroyed") } },
    13→    format: { with: /\A[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,62}\z/, message: "must be lowercase alphanumeric" }
    14→  validates :ssh_port, presence: true,
    15→    uniqueness: { conditions: -> { where.not(status: "destroyed") } },
    16→    inclusion: { in: SSH_PORT_RANGE }
    17→  validates :status, inclusion: { in: %w[pending running stopped destroyed] }
    18→  validates :image, presence: true
    19→  validates :vnc_geometry, inclusion: { in: VNC_GEOMETRIES }
    20→  validates :vnc_depth, inclusion: { in: VNC_DEPTHS }
    21→
    22→  scope :active, -> { where.not(status: "destroyed") }
    23→  scope :running, -> { where(status: "running") }
    24→
    25→  before_validation :assign_ssh_port, on: :create
    26→
    27→  # Turbo Streams for real-time UI updates
    28→  after_create_commit :broadcast_prepend_to_dashboard
    29→  after_update_commit :broadcast_replace_to_dashboard
    30→  after_destroy_commit :broadcast_remove_from_dashboard
    31→
    32→  def full_name
    33→    "#{user.name}-#{name}"
    34→  end
    35→
    36→  def connect_command(host: ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost"))
    37→    "ssh -p #{ssh_port} #{user.name}@#{host}"
    38→  end
    39→
    40→  def routed?
    41→    routes.loaded? ? routes.any? : routes.exists?
    42→  end
    43→
    44→  def temp?
    45→    temporary?
    46→  end
    47→
    48→  # Job lifecycle management
    49→  def job_in_progress?
    50→    job_status.present?
    51→  end
    52→
    53→  def job_failed?
    54→    job_error.present?
    55→  end
    56→
    57→  def start_job(status)
    58→    update!(job_status: status, job_started_at: Time.current, job_error: nil)
    59→  end
    60→
    61→  def finish_job
    62→    update!(job_status: nil, job_started_at: nil)
    63→  end
    64→
    65→  def fail_job(error_message)
    66→    update!(job_status: nil, job_error: error_message, job_started_at: nil)
    67→  end
    68→
    69→  private
    70→
    71→  def broadcast_prepend_to_dashboard
    72→    broadcast_prepend_to(
    73→      [user, "dashboard"],
    74→      partial: "dashboard/sandbox",
    75→      locals: { sandbox: self },
    76→      target: "sandboxes"
    77→    )
    78→  end
    79→
    80→  def broadcast_replace_to_dashboard
    81→    # If sandbox is destroyed, remove it from the dashboard instead of replacing
    82→    if status == "destroyed"
    83→      broadcast_remove_to([user, "dashboard"], target: dom_id(self))
    84→    else
    85→      broadcast_replace_to(
    86→        [user, "dashboard"],
    87→        partial: "dashboard/sandbox",
    88→        locals: { sandbox: self },
    89→        target: dom_id(self)
    90→      )
    91→    end
    92→  end
    93→
    94→  def broadcast_remove_from_dashboard
    95→    broadcast_remove_to([user, "dashboard"], target: dom_id(self))
    96→  end
    97→
    98→  def assign_ssh_port
    99→    return if ssh_port.present?
   100→
   101→    used_ports = Sandbox.where.not(status: "destroyed").pluck(:ssh_port)
   102→    available = SSH_PORT_RANGE.to_a - used_ports
   103→    self.ssh_port = available.first || raise("No SSH ports available")
   104→  end
   105→end
   106→
