```
     1→class SnapshotsController < ApplicationController
     2→  before_action :set_snapshot, only: %i[destroy clone]
     3→
     4→  def index
     5→    authorize Snapshot
     6→    @snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)
     7→  end
     8→
     9→  # POST /sandboxes/:id/snapshot
    10→  def create_for_sandbox
    11→    # Member route provides sandbox id as params[:id]
    12→    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])
    13→    authorize sandbox, :snapshot?
    14→
    15→    layers = params[:layers].present? ? Array(params[:layers]) : nil
    16→
    17→    SandboxManager.new.create_snapshot(
    18→      sandbox: sandbox,
    19→      name: params.require(:name),
    20→      label: params[:label].presence,
    21→      layers: layers,
    22→      data_subdir: params[:data_subdir].presence
    23→    )
    24→
    25→    redirect_to sandbox_path(sandbox), notice: "Snapshot created."
    26→  rescue SandboxManager::Error => e
    27→    redirect_back_or_to root_path, alert: "Failed to create snapshot: #{e.message}"
    28→  end
    29→
    30→  def destroy
    31→    SandboxManager.new.destroy_snapshot(user: Current.user, name: params[:name])
    32→    redirect_back_or_to root_path, notice: "Snapshot deleted."
    33→  rescue SandboxManager::Error => e
    34→    redirect_back_or_to root_path, alert: "Failed to delete snapshot: #{e.message}"
    35→  end
    36→
    37→  # POST /snapshots/:name/clone — redirect to new sandbox form pre-filled with snapshot
    38→  def clone
    39→    authorize @snapshot, :show?
    40→    redirect_to new_sandbox_path(snapshot: @snapshot.name)
    41→  end
    42→
    43→  private
    44→
    45→  def set_snapshot
    46→    @snapshot = Snapshot.find_by!(user: Current.user, name: params[:name])
    47→    authorize @snapshot
    48→  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound
    49→    SandboxManager.new.import_legacy_snapshots(Current.user)
    50→    @snapshot = Snapshot.find_by!(user: Current.user, name: params[:name])
    51→    authorize @snapshot
    52→  end
    53→end
    54→
```

```
   210→  # layers: array of "container", "home", "data", "workspace"
   211→  #   nil means "all available" based on sandbox config
   212→  def create_snapshot(sandbox:, name:, label: nil, layers: nil, data_subdir: nil)
   213→    raise Error, "Sandbox has no running container" if sandbox.container_id.blank?
   214→
   215→    user = sandbox.user
   216→    name = name.presence || Date.today.iso8601
   217→    requested_layers = layers&.map(&:to_s) || %w[container home data workspace]
   218→
   219→    snap = Snapshot.new(
   220→      user: user,
   221→      name: name,
   222→      label: label,
   223→      source_sandbox: sandbox.name,
   224→      data_subdir: data_subdir
   225→    )
   226→
   227→    # ── Container layer ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   228→    if requested_layers.include?("container")
   229→      repo = "sc-snap-#{user.name}"
   230→      container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   231→      image = container.commit(
   232→        repo: repo,
   233→        tag: name,
   234→        comment: "sandbox:#{sandbox.name}"
   235→      )
   236→      snap.docker_image = "#{repo}:#{name}"
   237→      snap.docker_size  = image.info["Size"]
   238→    end
   239→
   240→    # ── Home layer (BTRFS only) ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   241→    if requested_layers.include?("home") && sandbox.mount_home? && BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
   242→      home_src  = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/home"
   243→      home_dest = "#{DATA_DIR}/snapshots/#{user.name}/#{name}/home"
   244→      if Dir.exist?(home_src)
   245→        BtrfsHelper.snapshot_subvolume(home_src, home_dest)
   246→        snap.home_snapshot = home_dest
   247→        snap.home_size     = BtrfsHelper.subvolume_size(home_dest)
   248→      end
   249→    end
   250→
   251→    # ── Data layer (BTRFS only) ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   252→    if requested_layers.include?("data") && sandbox.data_path.present? && BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
   253→      if data_subdir.present?
   254→        data_src  = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}/#{data_subdir}".chomp("/")
   255→      else
   256→        data_src  = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}".chomp("/")
   257→      end
   258→      data_dest = "#{DATA_DIR}/snapshots/#{user.name}/#{name}/data"
   259→      if Dir.exist?(data_src)
   260→        BtrfsHelper.snapshot_subvolume(data_src, data_dest)
   261→        snap.data_snapshot = data_dest
   262→        snap.data_size     = BtrfsHelper.subvolume_size(data_dest)
   263→      end
   264→    end
   265→
   266→    # ── Workspace layer (BTRFS only) ─────────────────────────────────────────
   267→    if requested_layers.include?("workspace") && sandbox.persistent_volume? && sandbox.volume_path.present? && BtrfsHelper.btrfs?
   268→      workspace_src  = sandbox.volume_path
   269→      workspace_dest = "#{DATA_DIR}/snapshots/#{user.name}/#{name}/workspace"
   270→      if Dir.exist?(workspace_src)
   271→        BtrfsHelper.snapshot_subvolume(workspace_src, workspace_dest)
   272→        # Store workspace alongside data_snapshot when no data layer taken
   273→        snap.data_snapshot ||= workspace_dest
   274→        snap.data_size     = BtrfsHelper.subvolume_size(workspace_dest)
   275→      end
   276→    end
   277→
   278→    snap.save!
   279→    snap
   280→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
   281→    raise Error, "Failed to create snapshot: #{e.message}"
   282→  rescue BtrfsHelper::Error => e
   283→    raise Error, "Failed to snapshot filesystem layer: #{e.message}"
   284→  end

   286→  # Legacy alias kept for backward compatibility (used by existing API endpoint).
   287→  def snapshot(sandbox:, name: nil, **opts)

   311→  def destroy_snapshot(user:, name:)
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/jobs/snapshot_create_job.rb",
  "content": "class SnapshotCreateJob < ApplicationJob\n  queue_as :default\n\n  def perform(sandbox_id:, name:, label: nil, layers: nil, data_subdir: nil)\n    sandbox = Sandbox.find(sandbox_id)\n\n    sandbox.start_job(\"snapshotting\")\n\n    SandboxManager.new.create_snapshot(\n      sandbox: sandbox,\n      name: name,\n      label: label,\n      layers: layers,\n      data_subdir: data_subdir\n    )\n\n    sandbox.finish_job\n\n  rescue => e\n    Rails.logger.error(\"SnapshotCreateJob failed: #{e.message}\\n#{e.backtrace.join(\"\\n\")}\")\n    sandbox.fail_job(\"Snapshot failed: #{e.message}\")\n    raise\n  end\nend\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/snapshots_controller.rb",
  "content": "class SnapshotsController < ApplicationController\n  before_action :set_snapshot, only: %i[destroy clone]\n\n  def index\n    authorize Snapshot\n    @snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)\n  end\n\n  # POST /sandboxes/:id/snapshot\n  def create_for_sandbox\n    # Member route provides sandbox id as params[:id]\n    sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])\n    authorize sandbox, :snapshot?\n\n    layers = params[:layers].present? ? Array(params[:layers]) : nil\n\n    SnapshotCreateJob.perform_later(\n      sandbox_id: sandbox.id,\n      name: params.require(:name),\n      label: params[:label].presence,\n      layers: layers,\n      data_subdir: params[:data_subdir].presence\n    )\n\n    redirect_back_or_to root_path, notice: \"Snapshot queued — it will appear shortly.\"\n  rescue ActionController::ParameterMissing => e\n    redirect_back_or_to root_path, alert: \"Missing parameter: #{e.param}\"\n  end\n\n  def destroy\n    SnapshotDestroyJob.perform_later(user_id: Current.user.id, snapshot_name: params[:name])\n    redirect_back_or_to root_path, notice: \"Snapshot deletion queued.\"\n  end\n\n  # POST /snapshots/:name/clone — redirect to new sandbox form pre-filled with snapshot\n  def clone\n    authorize @snapshot, :show?\n    redirect_to new_sandbox_path(snapshot: @snapshot.name)\n  end\n\n  private\n\n  def set_snapshot\n    @snapshot = Snapshot.find_by!(user: Current.user, name: params[:name])\n    authorize @snapshot\n  rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound\n    SandboxManager.new.import_legacy_snapshots(Current.user)\n    @snapshot = Snapshot.find_by!(user: Current.user, name: params[:name])\n    authorize @snapshot\n  end\nend\n"
}
```

> AGENT

Done. Here's what changed:

**Removed "Close" button** — the indigo "browser" pill still shows when a VNC session is active, but there's no longer a close button on the dashboard card.

**Snapshot create → background job** (`SnapshotCreateJob`):
- Sets `sandbox.job_status = "snapshotting"` so the card shows the pulsing "Snapshotting..." badge and disables all action buttons while in progress
- Runs `docker commit` + BTRFS layer snapshots in the worker
- On success: `sandbox.finish_job`; on failure: `sandbox.fail_job(...)` with the error message
- Controller redirects immediately with *"Snapshot queued — it will appear shortly."*

**Snapshot delete → background job** (`SnapshotDestroyJob`):
- Fire-and-forget: removes Docker image and BTRFS subvolumes in the worker
- Authorization check still runs in the controller's `before_action :set_snapshot` before enqueuing
- Controller redirects immediately with *"Snapshot deletion queued."*

```
    78→# Start virtual X + VNC server for browser access.
    79→# Xvnc (TigerVNC) combines Xvfb and a VNC server in a single process and sends
    80→# the RFB banner immediately on connect — required for websockify compatibility.
    81→# x11vnc 0.9.17+ waits for client data before sending the banner, deadlocking
    82→# with websockify which also waits for the server to speak first.
    83→# Both processes run as $USERNAME (not root) for proper display ownership.
    84→VNC_ENABLED="${SANDCASTLE_VNC_ENABLED:-1}"
    85→VNC_GEOMETRY="${SANDCASTLE_VNC_GEOMETRY:-1280x900}"
    86→VNC_DEPTH="${SANDCASTLE_VNC_DEPTH:-24}"
    87→
    88→if command -v Xvnc &>/dev/null && [ "$VNC_ENABLED" = "1" ]; then
    89→    touch /var/log/xvnc.log /var/log/openbox.log
    90→    chown "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" /var/log/xvnc.log /var/log/openbox.log
    91→    su -s /bin/bash "$USERNAME" -c \
    92→        "Xvnc :99 -rfbport 5900 -SecurityTypes None -AlwaysShared -geometry ${VNC_GEOMETRY} -depth ${VNC_DEPTH} &>/var/log/xvnc.log &"
    93→    # Start Openbox window manager once the display is ready
    94→    if command -v openbox &>/dev/null; then
    95→        su -s /bin/bash "$USERNAME" -c \
    96→            'DISPLAY=:99 openbox &>/var/log/openbox.log &'
    97→    fi
    98→fi
```

> AGENT

`/etc/environment` is the right place — PAM reads it for every SSH login, making `DISPLAY=:99` available in both interactive and non-interactive sessions.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_016m8SCm4yY4ejX8siJaN9wk
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/images/sandbox/entrypoint.sh",
  "old_string": "if command -v Xvnc &>/dev/null && [ \"$VNC_ENABLED\" = \"1\" ]; then\n    touch /var/log/xvnc.log /var/log/openbox.log\n    chown \"$USERNAME:$USERNAME\" /var/log/xvnc.log /var/log/openbox.log\n    su -s /bin/bash \"$USERNAME\" -c \\\n        \"Xvnc :99 -rfbport 5900 -SecurityTypes None -AlwaysShared -geometry ${VNC_GEOMETRY} -depth ${VNC_DEPTH} &>/var/log/xvnc.log &\"\n    # Start Openbox window manager once the display is ready\n    if command -v openbox &>/dev/null; then\n        su -s /bin/bash \"$USERNAME\" -c \\\n            'DISPLAY=:99 openbox &>/var/log/openbox.log &'\n    fi\nfi",
  "new_string": "if command -v Xvnc &>/dev/null && [ \"$VNC_ENABLED\" = \"1\" ]; then\n    touch /var/log/xvnc.log /var/log/openbox.log\n    chown \"$USERNAME:$USERNAME\" /var/log/xvnc.log /var/log/openbox.log\n    su -s /bin/bash \"$USERNAME\" -c \\\n        \"Xvnc :99 -rfbport 5900 -SecurityTypes None -AlwaysShared -geometry ${VNC_GEOMETRY} -depth ${VNC_DEPTH} &>/var/log/xvnc.log &\"\n    # Start Openbox window manager once the display is ready\n    if command -v openbox &>/dev/null; then\n        su -s /bin/bash \"$USERNAME\" -c \\\n            'DISPLAY=:99 openbox &>/var/log/openbox.log &'\n    fi\n    # Export DISPLAY for all SSH sessions via PAM environment\n    echo \"DISPLAY=:99\" >> /etc/environment\nfi"
}
```

> AGENT

Done. When VNC is enabled, `DISPLAY=:99` is appended to `/etc/environment` at container startup. PAM reads that file for every SSH session (interactive and non-interactive), so users can run any GUI app directly without needing `export DISPLAY=:99` themselves.
